Special handling for different features\n    if si_threshold is None:\n        return value\n\n    # Check if value appears to be in SI units (outside normal range)\n    # Different logic for different types of conversions\n\n    if feature_name in ['Serum_Creatinine']:\n        # Special handling for different features\n    if si_threshold is None:\n        return value\n\n    # Check if value appears to be in SI units (outside normal range)\n    # Different logic for different types of conversions\n\n    if feature_name in ['Serum_Creatinine']:\n        #
